# Aegis PMA — CIO brief for Thu 29 Oct 2026

- **Session:** Thursday 29 Oct US cash session, opens 21:30 SGT (US on daylight time until 1 Nov). Core PCE, GDP and initial jobless claims print at 08:30 ET = 20:30 SGT, one hour before the open.
- **Data:** AQE to the Wed 28 Oct close (export dated Thu 29 Oct 2026, 08:51 SGT). FOMC decided at 14:00 ET inside that session, so every row carries the post-FOMC close. Tiger book read 04:14Z, read-only.
- **Room:** 52 names, 10 voters: 5 ADVANCE, 38 HOLD-FOR-CONDITIONS (including the held V), 9 PASS (including the held MP: PASS on any add).
- **How the run was made:** it sat on local disposable disk until PUBLISH (no mid-run checkpoint pushes, by design); seats ran as general-purpose agents reading their own card and packet by path; the fundamentals consult was consulted only and carries no vote. Every declared item is in the table under the verdict table.
- **Late declaration (conductor, after WRITE):** the alert-levels tool refused V (held, HOLD-FOR-CONDITIONS on the add question) because the card builder only writes an add card for a held name on ADVANCE. Fixed in the card file with a stated reason (no shared buy level; the broker stop 391.80 is the exit), so AQE watches V as a held name only. The code fix belongs in pma_cards.py.

- Door is how the name reached the vote. Lane: a MOMENTUM verdict was decided by the strength voices only, a PULLBACK verdict by the whole room.
- Valen setup is the best grade the VIV handbook's own checklists give the name ("—" when none is graded; (+n) counts further grades of lesser status). U&R is AQE's short reclaim read: MET = the last session was the reclaim day, SETUP = the reclaim day is today's session.
- ⛔ marks an ADVANCE or HOLD on Valen's no-buy list: BRZE (Valen's label: 6.8 ATR above its 20-day), GMAB (7.3 ATR) and NTSK (4.3 ATR). No ADVANCE is on the list, and today carries no market-wide no-buy label. Two PASS names are also on it (CWK and YOU, below the 200-day).
- Seated by rule, not by any voice: CART (ADVANCE), BRZE, GMAB, NTSK, DXC, ASAN, TENB (HOLD) and BBWI, ACN, CCI (PASS) were seated for strength; YOU, NXPI (PASS) and NCNO (HOLD) sit on reserved reclaim seats. No voice nominated any of them.
- HOLD means HOLD-FOR-CONDITIONS. Levels are from the run's condition file; "1h candle" is the hourly bar.

## Run declarations (every declared item, in plain words)

| # | Declaration | In plain words |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Data | AQE export dated Thu 29 Oct 08:51 SGT carries the Wed 28 Oct close (FOMC day). 215 daily-list names, 0 flagged by the export's own data-quality guard, U&R as of 28 Oct on every row. Engine run status success; voice packets 12 of 12. |
| 2 | Ledgers | The verdict and phase-4 ledgers were read from the canonical AQE main path (the 28 Oct record was pushed in commit da7c709 on the PM's approval). No ledger-source degradation today. |
| 3 | No mid-run checkpoint pushes, by design | The default sync paths include the data folder that rebuilds the HF Space, so the run sat on local disposable disk until PUBLISH. One consolidated push at PUBLISH under aegis/output/pma/ only, with read-back verification. |
| 4 | Canonical record push held | The canonical record paths for 29 Oct (reports, data/pma) are held for the PM's yes, as on 28 Oct (open item 1). |
| 5 | Seat transport | Seats ran as general-purpose agents reading their own card and packet by path. The fundamentals consult is consulted only (R22): ten voters. |
| 6 | Fundamentals pack | 141 names: 121 carried from the 28 Oct pack plus 20 newly seated names pulled fresh on 29 Oct after ranking; 0 gaps. GMAB's 2025 growth row is in USD while earlier rows are DKK, so its 2025 revenue growth is distorted; EQH and CCI carry negative-equity ratios. Left as returned. |
| 7 | Challenge forms outside the figure audit | No validate mode exists for them and they were not hand-traced today; their figures are the challengers' own reading of their packets. |
| 8 | Vote packet size | 130,531 bytes against a soft limit of 70,000 (28 Oct was 133,534). Normal; not trimmed. |
| 9 | Broker read (read-only, 04:14Z) | V 38 sh stop-limit 391.80 / 390.80 and MP 300 sh stop-limit 36.66 / 36.66, both good till cancelled. No entry was taken in the Wed 28 Oct US session: no Aegis fills in Tiger (the only fills are Project MD options, not Aegis). |
| 10 | Scorecard | Nine runs (15 to 28 Oct) graded as of the 28 Oct close, horizon 5 sessions, beta cap 2.5. 573 symbols; 28 Oct closes from the export (215) plus 357 single-symbol pulls; ACA returned no row (null). 0 rows refused, 40 not graded. |
| 11 | Repeat-watch ledger gaps | 398 rows for dates that predate the verdict ledger (normal). |
| 12 | Packets | No stamp file shipped on main; checksums recorded at prepare. |
| 13 | Inbox clean | 18 AQE threads trashed, 0 remaining. Two GitHub notifications, "Run failed: AQE voice packets sync - main" for 9620f43 and for da7c709, were left untouched by rule and not checked (open item 4). |
| 14 | Purity | Rank invariance PASS. Crowding audit: the top five by agreement are 0% bracket-valid against 21% across all 14 qualifiers; no sign that consensus tracks bracket availability. |
| 15 | Seat notes | One voice's first part-1 ballot was refused for writing a threshold as a figure and was re-filed with no figure changed. One voice card says trend strength (ADX) is not served while the packet now carries it (card stale; level used, slope declared not served). Two voices note the 40-day average, CCI and the 5-day Elder series are not served on the held rows MP and V. |
| 16 | Lens feed gap | Seven names (HOG, EOG, CDW, CSX, BRZE, GMAB, BBWI) read blank on four of six lenses: a feed gap, not agreement. |
| 17 | AQE text defects (checked in the Crown artifact) | Both hold. read_me_first says the size is cut "because the trend is crowded" and that "a lot of money is already leaning the same way", while trend funds show 0 of 18 markets at an extreme (dial 1.00) and the size note shows the cut is the family cap ("CTA dial 1.00 -> capped at 0.50"). The QQQ call wall prints at 720, the same strike as the put wall and below spot 725.61, although its text calls it the positioning above the market. Neither changes a number in the brief; the QQQ walls are treated as low quality. |

## §0A EXECUTIVE CONSENSUS — WHY THESE ADVANCE

### The book in three bullets

- **What the five share.** All five are long equity that beat SPY over 20 days (from +6.86 points for EMR to +34.21 for CART), four of the five sit within 1.5% of a 52-week high (CART is 4.04% under), and none has an earnings date on record. They spread across four sectors: Consumer Discretionary (HOG, CART), Energy (CNQ), Industrials (EMR), Technology (CDW).
- **Where agreement was strongest and thinnest.** Strongest: CNQ and CART (unanimous among the strength voices) and HOG and CDW (broad, no objection). Thinnest: EMR, near-unanimous across the whole room but carried over one real objection (it does not lead the market and broke out on 0.91x volume).
- **What invalidates the group.** SPY losing its 750 put wall (2.89% below spot 772.31) on a hot Core PCE or GDP print at 20:30 SGT: dealers are short gamma with SPY 0.16% under its 773.51 flip, so the floor going would hit all five at once.

| MP | turnaround | NEUTRAL | HELD. Rare-earth turnaround, carried from 2026-10-21 pack: rev_growth_2025 0.10 after -0.20/-0.52, net_margin -0.199, fcf_yield -0.073, de 0.48. sma_distance_pct -15.44: C22 needs the story verified intact. |
| YOU | fast grower | SUPPORT | Cash-generative grower: rev_growth 0.17/0.26/0.40, pe 30.1, fcf_yield 0.109 (meets C13 terrific line), dy 0.017, de 0.53. eps_growth_2025 -0.37 follows a tax-boosted prior year; growth is decelerating. |
| NXPI | cyclical | NEUTRAL | Semis cyclical near a trough: rev_growth -0.03/-0.05, eps_growth -0.19/-0.09, pe 19.7, de 0.91, dy 0.016, fcf_yield 0.058. C14 inventory-vs-sales (the cyclical tell) not served. |
| NCNO | fast grower | CONTRADICT | Growth slowing at a high multiple: rev_growth 0.10/0.13/0.17, pe 73.1, net_margin 0.054, fcf_yield 0.055. C17: deceleration is the fast-grower sell signal; PEG about 7 on revenue. |
| BRZE | fast grower | NEUTRAL | Sales grower slowing, still losing money: rev_growth 0.24/0.26/0.33, net_margin -0.136, pe -44.5, de 0.16. sma_distance_pct 49.59 means the price is far ahead of any earnings. |
| GMAB | fast grower | NEUTRAL | Biotech royalty grower: rev_growth_2024 0.31, eps_growth_2024 0.84, pe 45.1, de 0.89, net_margin 0.082. rev_growth_2025 -0.83 is the DKK-to-USD switch, not a collapse. sma_distance_pct 58.27. |
| NTSK | turnaround | CONTRADICT | Deep losses and heavy leverage: net_margin -0.918, op_margin -0.947, de 4.67, pb 62.73, fcf_yield -0.007, rev_growth 0.32/0.32. Fails the C12 debt line; no earnings to value. |
| DXC | turnaround | NEUTRAL | Shrinking IT-services turnaround: rev_growth -0.02/-0.06/-0.05, pb 0.86, pe 21.2, de 1.37. fcf_yield 0.487 would clear the C13 20% line; the figure needs checking. eps_growth_2026 -0.95. |
| ASAN | fast grower | CONTRADICT | Growth fading, still losing money: rev_growth 0.09/0.11/0.19, net_margin -0.186, pe -18.2, de 2.48, pb 26.59. A C17 deceleration with no earnings floor under it. |
| BBWI | slow grower | NEUTRAL | Cheap, shrinking, levered: pe 5.9, fcf_yield 0.233, dy 0.036, rev_growth 0.0/-0.02/-0.02, eps_growth_2026 -0.15, pb -4.21 (negative equity). Cash is real; there is no growth. |
| CART | stalwart | SUPPORT | Profitable platform: rev_growth 0.11/0.11/0.19, eps_growth_2024 1.14, pe 29.7, de 0.01, fcf_yield 0.088, net_margin 0.12. Debt-free with FCF near the C13 line; a fair price. |
| TENB | fast grower | CONTRADICT | Near-zero earnings at an extreme multiple: pe 712.3, net_margin 0.006, rev_growth 0.11/0.13/0.17, de 2.12. fcf_yield 0.055 is the support; C17 deceleration. |
| ACN | stalwart | SUPPORT | Stalwart at a reasonable price: rev_growth 0.06/0.07/0.01, eps_growth 0.11/0.06/0.06, pe 18.3, dy 0.026, de 0.42, fcf_yield 0.076. Good recession defence; slow growth. |
| CCI | asset play | NEUTRAL | Tower REIT after divestiture: rev_growth_2025 -0.35, eps_growth_2025 1.11 off a loss, dy 0.048, pe 36.6. pb -10.92 and de -6.69 (negative equity) make C12 unreadable. Asset value at spot not served. |

- **room / tally_leaders:** None of the four 3-seat names (HOG, TMO, CNQ, EMR) gets a fundamental SUPPORT. HOG CONTRADICTS: sales and earnings have fallen two years running and FCF is negative. TMO and EMR are stalwarts priced well above their growth. CNQ is a cyclical with sound books, but its low P/E follows an EPS jump.
- **room / earnings_absent:** Eight names have no meaningful earnings, so PEG cannot be computed: TEAM, GTLB, BRZE, ASAN, NTSK and SYNA lose money, and IOT and TENB earn close to nothing. Lynch buys earnings; here the price carries the story and the figures do not back it.
- **room / growth_at_reasonable_price:** The figures support six names where growth and price line up: SLDE, PGR, LLY, SN, GLBE and CART. SLDE and PGR are insurers, so their margins are underwriting-cycle margins, which the PM should confirm.
- **room / fast_grower_deceleration:** In nine software and internet names, revenue growth has slowed three years in a row. C17 names deceleration as the fast-grower sell signal. It is a watch item, not a gate.
- **room / premium_stalwarts_shrinking:** Four stalwarts are priced as growers while their earnings shrink. DHR and CSX have had falling EPS for three years. SNPS earnings fell 45% at 84x. ILMN's sales have shrunk three years at 53x.
- **held / MP_C22_proviso:** MP is held and trades below its average. C22 says a 25% drawdown is a buying chance ONLY if the story is verified intact. Revenue is growing again, but it still loses money and burns cash. I cannot verify the story; the PM must. I defer to the broker stop 36.66 (house law, C21 overridden).
- **held / V_C20_rotation:** V is held and its story is intact on the figures. C20 says rotate on valuation into a cheaper stalwart, never on a gain alone. The P/E history needed to call it fully valued is not served. I defer to the broker stop 391.80.
- **room / data_distortions:** Figures that mislead if read raw: GMAB 2025 revenue (currency switch); EQH, CCI, BBWI negative equity (C12 unreadable); NTNX, FRSH one-off earnings (net margin above operating margin); DXC fcf_yield 0.487 needs checking; CRBG, SHO, SN, V, MP carried from the 2026-10-21 pack.
- **room / C23_window:** 2026-10-29 falls in the October-December tax-loss window (C23). Nearly every room name trades above its average, so the window applies only to held MP. It is context, never a nomination.
- **room / cyclical_inventory_blind:** There are seven cyclicals in the room: CNQ, EOG, XOM, LNG, NXPI, HOG and CWK. Their deciding test, inventory versus sales (C14), is not served, so each cyclical read is provisional.

#### Macro seats

- **crown:** Broad, calm index sitting on a knife-edge: SPY and QQQ 0.16% under dealer gamma flip, single-stock vol gap wide and widening. Family = hidden-stress downside at 0.50x, but contested by benign breadth. Size 0.5x (Family cap 0.50x on the PM's own risk budget. Trend-fund dial is 1.00 (no crowding cut) - the 0.50 comes from the family cap, not from crowded trend funds.)
- **druckenmiller:** sizing tone light. The macro wind is constructive, but a momentum book enters into: sector momentum fading in all 11 sectors, mega-cap leadership breaking, negative dealer gamma at the flip, single-stock vol gap elevated and rising, and Core PCE + GDP printing today. Constructive weather does not excuse buying late-stage momentum into an event with amplified moves. Weather only - gates nothing.
